([neckarshore-skills/obsidian-vault-autopilot](https://github.com/neckarshore-skills/obsidian-vault-autopilot/tree/HEAD/skills/note-rename) · ★3)

- **note-review**: Review one markdown note through retrieval practice — quiz the user from memory first, then reveal what their recall and the note itself are missing. Use when the user wants to check a note's completeness or their retention of it. Trigger on "review this note", "note review", "test me on my note", "is this note complete", "did I capture everything". Never open with a critique or summary of the note — the questions always come first. ([atalariq/skills](https://github.com/atalariq/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/learning/note-review) · ★1)

- **note-revision**: Merge new information into an existing note without losing what it already says — classify the change, re-synthesize State, append to Timeline, and preview before writing. - **note-routing**: Decides the destination directory and filename for a note using entity schema.directory values and the workspace's RESOLVER.md; use whenever a new or moved file needs a home before it is written. ([ebridges/wakil](https://github.com/ebridges/wakil/tree/HEAD/src/wakil/skills/note-routing))
- **note-search**: Hybrid semantic + keyword search over CrudusLiv's vault. Use whenever you need to recall something said before or find similar past drafts for voice-matching. ([CrudusLiv/Vesper](https://github.com/CrudusLiv/Vesper/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/note-search))
- **note-search**: MANDATORY retrieval interface for any markdown vault note lookup. Use this skill — never grep, ls, find, or manual directory listing — whenever: a note path, title, or topic is mentioned; the user asks what notes exist on a subject; a role needs context before reading or editing notes; a concept needs to be discovered in the vault; backlinks or linked notes are needed; or any search over the vault is required. Skipping this skill and using grep/ls/find instead causes catastrophic context bloat in large vaults. Even a simple "do I have notes on X?" MUST go through this skill. ([ygtkayali/Atlas-Workflow-System](https://github.com/ygtkayali/Atlas-Workflow-System/tree/HEAD/shared/skills/note-search))

Write descriptive prose mainly in Chinese, preserve key English terms and important English descriptive source sentences, and preserve original answers and complete concept definitions.
